<script>
	window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {

		$(document).ready(function() {

			// Update Language toggle button
			var toggleLink = $('.toggle-link-data')
			var toggleLangLink = toggleLink.attr('data-language-link');
			var toggleLangShortText = toggleLink.attr('data-language-short-text');
			var desktopNavLangToggle = $('.main-nav-lang-toggle');
			var mobileNavLangToggle = $('.mobile-nav-lang-toggle a');
			var footerNavLangToggle = $('.footer-nav-lang-toggle');

			if (desktopNavLangToggle.length > 0) {
				// Desktop
				//Change the text only if it is different
				if (toggleLangShortText != desktopNavLangToggle.text()) {
					desktopNavLangToggle.text(toggleLangShortText);
				}
				// Change the link only if it is different
				if (toggleLangLink != desktopNavLangToggle.attr('href')) {
					desktopNavLangToggle.attr('href', toggleLangLink);
				}
			}

			if (mobileNavLangToggle.length > 0 && toggleLangLink != mobileNavLangToggle.attr('href')) {
				//Change the mobile link only if it is different
				mobileNavLangToggle.attr('href', toggleLangLink);
			}

			if (footerNavLangToggle.length > 0) {
				footerNavLangToggle.attr('href', toggleLangLink);
			}
		});

			angular.module('fca.core.competitionCompare', []);
	});
</script>

In the rest of Canada, Purchase Prices include Consumer Cash Discounts and other incentives (where applicable) and exclude freight ($1,895 - $2,595), air conditioning charge, licence, insurance, any retailer administration fees and other retailer charges (if applicable) and other applicable fees and taxes. Retailers may sell for less. See participating retailers for complete details. <br/><br/></p>
